# AI Role

Inputs and outputs for the AI Role pass. Inputs: Symbol, Company, Previous sub-sectors (from the earlier pipeline, with its A/B fit tier), Description (from the screener), News AI (from the news pass). Outputs: Sub-sectors, AI Role. Rows 131 onward (companies added November 25, 2026): News AI is copied in from `News Summary.md`.

It raised its dividend 17%. |
